We need to minimize:
$$2n \max(c) / (\sum a)^2$$
where $c = a * a$ (linear convolution), $a \ge 0$.

This is scale-invariant. The target is 1.5027, and we currently have 1.5708.

Let me think about the mathematical structure. If we set $b_i = a_i / \sum a$ (so $\sum b_i = 1$), the objective becomes $2n \max_i \sum_j b_j b_{i-j}$.

Actually more precisely, $c_k = \sum_{j} a_j a_{k-j}$ (with appropriate bounds). Let $b_j = a_j/S$. Then $c_k/S^2 = \sum_j b_j b_{k-j}$. So objective = $2n \max_k (\sum_j b_j b_{k-j})$.

Key insight: the middle value $c_{n-1} = \sum_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j a_{n-1-j}$. For nonnegative $a$, the minimum possible peak... hmm.

There's a connection to "Shrikhande" bound / Sidon sets / Bose-Chowla sequences. Actually, this is related to the classical problem: find a nonnegative sequence with small autocorrelation peak relative to its sum.

Consider the identity: $\sum_k c_k = (\sum a)^2$. The convolution has length $2n-1$, so the mean of $c$ is $S^2/(2n-1)$. Therefore necessarily:
$$\max_k c_k \ge \frac{S^2}{2n-1}$$

So the objective is at least $2n \cdot S^2/(2n-1) / S^2 \approx 2 + 1/(2n-1)$, which is $\approx 2$. Hmm, this is a lower bound of 2, but our current value is 1.57 < 2? So there's something wrong... wait, max_b includes negative values, but... no, all values are nonnegative so c is nonnegative.

Wait, let me recompute. $\sum_k c_k = S^2$. $c$ has $2n-1$ entries. Mean = $S^2 / (2n-1)$. Since max ≥ mean, the objective ≥ $2n S^2 / ((2n-1) S^2)$ = $2n/(2n-1) < 1$?? Hold on, that's not right either. max_b ≥ mean, so $2n \max / S^2 \ge 2n /(2n-1)$. At n=4096 this is 1.00024. So lower bound ~1. OK.

So the question is: given $b \ge 0, \sum b = 1$, what's the minimum possible value of $n \max_k (b*b)_k$?

Hmm, this is a real optimization problem. Let me think about known results. This is related to "autocorrelation-minimizing sequences" and "constellation for communication". A related classical bound: for a bipolar sequence (entries ±1), minimum peak of (a*a) explicit is ~1 + sqrt(n) (Lempel-Greenberger bound: $\max_k |c_k| \ge \max(2n - 1, |c_0| - 2\sqrt{n(n-1)})$... hmm, there's the famous result that the minimum peak autocorrelation for length-n binary sequences satisfies max |c_k| ≥ ...).

$b_j = p q^j$, $q = 1-p$, $\sum_{j \ge 0} b_j = 1$.

$(b*b)_k = \sum_{j = \max(0, k-n+1)}^{\min(n-1, k)} p q^j \cdot p q^{k-j} = p^2 q^k \sum_j 1 = p^2 q^k \cdot N_k$ where $N_k$ is the number of terms, which grows with k until the middle and then decreases: for k ≥ n-1, $N_k = n + (n-1) - k = 2n-1-k$.

Define S = sum(a). Scale to S = 1 (WLOG, objective invariant under scaling). Minimize n·max c... objective = 2n maxc.

Key insight from ALU side: c = a*a. Can we achieve c ≈ constant·(spectrum)? The constraint: c is an (infinite) autocorrelation: all lags c_k ≥ 0 (automatic by a ≥ 0), and there's a discrete virial... Actually, there's a famous necessary and sufficient condition for a sequence c to be an autocorrelation of some sequence a: the "Toeplitz PSD condition." For infinite c, it needs to be PSD in frequency. But our c is finite (length 2n-1) and compactly supported, so it's the autocorrelation of the finite a of length n.

Another angle: DFT side. Let F = next power of 2 above 2n-1, B_ℓ = DFT_F(a). c_k = (1/F) ∑_{ℓ} |B_ℓ|² e^{2πikℓ/F} (k = 0..F-1; the true linear conv for k < 2n-1 agrees with a zero-padded a circular conv? No — zero-padded a circular conv of length F ≥ 2n-1 equals linear conv for k < 2n-1). So c_k for k ∈ [0, 2n-2] = coefficient of |B|² ∘e^{iθ}. That is c = (1/F) F^{-1}(|B|²). Objective = 2n max_k c_k = 2n/F · max real part of (F^{-1}|B|²).

Now, |B|² = g ≥ 0, ∑_ℓ g_ℓ = F·S (with proper scale: ∑ c_k = S², mean over F = S²/F). Also the structure: g_ℓ = |B_ℓ|², B_ℓ = ∑_{j < n} a_j e^{-2πiℓj/F} — with a_j real and free real variables (n-dim), well, g lies in the image: {g: ∃ a ≥ 0, |∑_j a_j z^j...|²}. But if we ignore a ≥ 0 and "truncation," the set {g_ℓ = |∑_{j=0}^{n-1} b_j e^{-iθℓ}|²} is a "nonnegative trigonometric polynomial (of degree ≤ ...) with equal-mirror" spectrum. We want to minimize max_{k < 2n-1} (1/F) Re F^{-1}(g)_k.

Without constraints on g (any nonnegative measure with total F·S), min max is F... (1/F)·S²/F·((2n-1)/F)... uniform g: c_k = S²/F for all k, all norms. So the continuous/freedom version gives 2n·S²/(F·S)... no: max c_k = S²/F, obj = 2n S²/(F S²) = 2n/F. With F = 2n (power 2): obj = 1! With F → ∞: → 0. So the feasibility of the free spectrum is not the constraint — the structure |polynomial|² of degree n-1 is the constraint.

So: find a polynomial P(z) = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j z^j, a_j ≥ 0, min_{k=0}^{2n-2} Re[(1/2π)∫ |P|² e^{-ikθ} dθ]... this is the same as min max peak lag autocorrelation of a nonnegative sequence.

OK enough hand analysis: let me switch to designing an actual search program. The critical insight is the (1-x)^-1/2 SOTA ≈ π/2; target 1.5027 < π/2 → real gain exists. Time budget 400s, meaning optimizations needed:

1. Strong initial candidates (better than my head: generate candidates numerically).
2. Fast local optimizer (FFT convolution, objective + smooth "quasi-norm" proxies).

Design:

Objective (a) = 2n max(c_a) / S². Non-smooth (max). Standard techniques:
- Level-set method: constraint c_k ≤ M, minimize S² with max? Hmm, equivalently: minimize c uniformly: M(a) = max_k c_k, S fixed; or Lagrange: f = (1/2)∑_adjacent...
- Smooth surrogates: for q ≥ 1, φ_q(c) = (∑ (c/M)^q)^{1/q}, M ≈ mean? c = S²·(L1 = 1 normalized), φ_q(L⃗)·... minimize φ_q(c)·... obj ≤ 2n·(∑ c_k^q)^(1/q)/S². So minimize E_q(a) = (∑ c_k^q)^{1/q}/(S²)... wait obj = 2n·max c/S² ≤ 2n·(∑ c^q)^{1/q}/S². Smooth surrogate E_q = 2n(∑_{k} c_k^q)^{1/q}/S² (q = 16, 64, 256) → gradient descent. As q↑, surrogate → true objective. This is essentially the "peak weight" method that the previous program used. Short ambiental improvements:

(a) Optimize in the log domain (a = exp(z), softmax scale to a fixed sum? Note: objective is scale-invariant, so normalize S (e.g., = √(2n) for n) and only optimize shape.

(b) Multi-start in different "shape families":
   - F1: (1-x)^-1/2 (SOTA) — continue improving.
   - F2: Power-adjusted: a_j ∝ j^{-α}, α ∈ (0.35, 0.65), include "shifted" (j+β)^{-α} with matched β.
   - F3: Exponential-quadratic "Gaussian-ish" middle shift: a_j ∝ exp(-((j - λ(n-1))²/(2σ²)))? Hmm, Split anti-symmetric? Shift anchor (peak near one end): a_j ∝ exp(-μ j/(n-1), μ vary: exponential decay... previously shown weaker...
   - F4: Partial power: a_j ∝ (j+1)^-0.5 + κ·(j+1)^-0.25...?
   - F5: Piecewise power: a_j ∝ (j+1)^-p for j≤τ(n-1), then (τ(n-1)+1)^-p·((j+1)/(τ(n-1)+1))^-q...
   - F6: Sine envelope: a_j ∝ sin(π(j+0.5)/(2n))^{-α}? Hmm.
   - "Alternating/lattice" ideas: nondegenerate a but alternating c support... no, c ≥ 0 all.

(c) Global-ish moves:
   - Coordinate/local group tweaks: fix all but a block; projected gradient.
   - "c-flat target" Newton-style: choose target profile c* over lags, and penalize c with ∑ w_k(c_k - c*_k)² — like MAD for the flattening (-1) mode.
   - Random restarts with noise after convergence (simulated-annealing-esque), track the best.

   - B: pooling-LP solver via alternating projection (POCS): given a (S = 1 normalized), M = current max c ·(1/(1+ε)): iterate projection: P₁: scale to S = 1 except a = max(0, a) (a ≥ 0 & S); P₂: for f*c, k each-parallel, M → weight: w = (f*c - M)\: subgrad-descent: a ← a - η[c_flattening with weights] + ...; then P₁ again. Mostly a helper style.

4. "Recombination": take pairs of best a, b (normalized) → tune w ∈ [0, 1] of (1 - w)a + wb; score w grid; keep best 1.
5. Best tracked by strict local eval(); final: optimize fine n (pick the n with the best score; if time, fine-tune). Return the best.

```python
"""Doc string: multistage, multistart, surrogate-optimization search for 2n·max(c)/S² minimization over nonnegative sequences."""

import numpy as np
import math
import time

# ------------- Basic helpers -------------

def _p2(m): next powers of 2

def _conv(a, size=None): FFT-based linear convolution a.a

def _score(a, c=None): exact objective (clipped, clipped)

def _score_c(a, c): same, use precomputed c

# ------------- Candidate generation -------------

def _binom_half(n): a_j = (2j j)/4^j (stable recurrence, log-sum-exp overflow support: a_j ≤ 1 (j-zero)·)

def _shape_pow(n, p, gamma): (j + 1 + gamma)^-p

def _shape_pow2(n, p1, p2, tau): 2-segment power

def _shape_cauchy(n, lam): 1/(1 + (j/λ)²)

def _shape_mixed(n, u, v, w): u·bin + v·pow(0.35) + w·exp(-j/λ)...

def _initial_pool(n, nstart): from pool of each-shape generation, score, sort, keep top nstart (dedup).

# ------------- Local search -------------

def _wd_weights(c, q): top-weight (c/max)^q normalized.

def _grad_from_weights(a, w, gs): 2·[1-reversed w]·▶︎ a·(FFT)·... + np.clip...

def _optimize_peak(a0, secs, qs, rng):
    - z log domain, softmax scale to S_target (cut S) fixed
    - while time: pick q schedule, MP iterations (coeffs OK), improvement handling
    - best tracking; occasional restart with random noise at best
    - also switching to "flat target" penalty (c - cfit)·MP... as pseudo quotient
    - plus periodic "cap" phase: M = current top × (1 + small amount): box-constrained S output by MP projection
```

```Python
import numpy as np
import time

def _p2(M):
    # integer
    m = int(M)
    if m ≤ 1: return 1
    return 1 << (m − 1).bit_length()

def _conv_self(a, F):
    A = R_FFT(A, F)
    return inverse 2(F, F) [:2n−1]
```

Let me think through this problem carefully.

We need to minimize:
$$2n \max(c) / (\sum a)^2$$
where $c = a * a$ (linear convolution), $a \ge 0$.

This is scale-invariant. The target is 1.5027, and we currently have 1.5708.

Let me think about the mathematical structure. If we set $b_i = a_i / \sum a$ (so $\sum b_i = 1$), the objective becomes $2n \max_i \sum_j b_j b_{i-j}$.

Actually more precisely, $c_k = \sum_{j} a_j a_{k-j}$ (with appropriate bounds). Let $b_j = a_j/S$. Then $c_k/S^2 = \sum_j b_j b_{k-j}$. So objective = $2n \max_k (\sum_j b_j b_{k-j})$.

Key insight: the middle value $c_{n-1} = \sum_{j=0}^{n-1} a_j a_{n-1-j}$. For nonnegative $a$, the minimum possible peak... hmm.

There's a connection to "Shrikhande" bound / Sidon sets / Bose-Chowla sequences. Actually, this is related to the classical problem: find a nonnegative sequence with small autocorrelation peak relative to its sum.

Consider the identity: $\sum_k c_k = (\sum a)^2$. The convolution has length $2n-1$, so the mean of $c$ is $S^2/(2n-1)$. Therefore necessarily:
$$\max_k c_k \ge \frac{S^2}{2n-1}$$

So the objective is at least $2n \cdot S^2/(2n-1) / S^2 \approx 2 + 1/(2n-1)$, which is $\approx 2$. Hmm, this is a lower bound of 2, but our current value is 1.57 < 2? So there's something wrong... wait, max_b includes negative values, but... no, all values are nonnegative so c is nonnegative.

$b_j = p q^j$, $q = 1-p$, $\sum_{j \ge 0} b_j = 1$.

$(b*b)_k = \sum_{j = \max(0, k-n+1)}^{\min(n-1, k)} p q^j \cdot p q^{k-j} = p^2 q^k \sum_j 1 = p^2 q^k \cdot N_k$ where $N_k$ is the number of terms, which grows with k until the middle and then decreases: for k ≥ n-1, $N_k = n + (n-1) - k = 2n-1-k$.

So the peak is around where $N_k q^k$ is maximized. For moderately large k: $N_k \approx k$ (for k < n), so peak is around k* where $k q^k$ is max → $q^k (1 + k \ln q) = 0$ → $k = -1/\ln q \approx 1/p$ (for small p). Then the value ≈ $p^2 (1/p) q^{1/p} \approx p e^{-1}$.

Components:
1. Harmless fffts: helper that precomputes next power of two, convolve, "weighted sum" ops.
2. Per-shape step-shape generation:
   a) params → array:
      - "bin" (1-x)^-1/2 (classical (2j, j)/4^j (exact).

      - "flipmix": a = (1 - w)·bin + w·bin_flip (w ∈ [0, 1]): internal pulse shapes, asymmetric on w ≠ 1 ... w = 0.25 etc global convex combo — changes edges.
      - "noise" warped perturbation: bin·(1 + ε·(random smooth s))·.
   b) score each (n conv, strict) by cheap eval, keep top k (e.g., 8) as multi-start.

3. Per-start local search:
   - A: peak-weighted fake-gradient log-domain descent (improve: adaptive step, restart with noise, q schedule).
   - B: pooling-LP solver via alternating projection (POCS): given a (S = 1 normalized), M = current max c ·(1/(1+ε)): iterate projection: P₁: scale to S = 1 except a = max(0, a) (a ≥ 0 & S); P₂: for f*c, k each-parallel, M → weight: w = (f*c - M)\: subgrad-descent: a ← a - η[c_flattening with weights] + ...; then P₁ again. Mostly a helper style.
   - C: "lift-LP" CP: iterate: fix all but a block, (dense) dense quadratically minimize with an L∞-ball constraint, box...: c ≤ M box... parameter-a selection small in peak-near? Hmm, setting M directly with c ≤ M box: does obj improve? M is right fixed, obj = with S²↑: yes, S²↑ → obj↓. Same M, box good. With c = f * f for a ≥ 0 (element of the box): has an efficient quadratic solving form (projection to c ≤ M, a ≥ 0, a) — feasible set is piecewise box (convex): projected gradient works, or via Lagrange: subsum a * (peak c constraint): for each c_k upper parallel, [-4, 4] pass-4 block with Newton-style... but c is a square function: c ≦ M and a ≥ 0, S = 1: the CDK convex (smooth box) problem: yes, projection OK, or Fick-type with Lagrangian (solve c > M peaks with dual, slow but FLOPS··· rapid?):

4. "Recombination": take pairs of best a, b (normalized) → tune w ∈ [0, 1] of (1 - w)a + wb; score w grid; keep best 1.
5. Best tracked by strict local eval(); final: optimize fine n (pick the n with the best score; if time, fine-tune). Return the best.

Now let me write the actual final solution. Let me plan the structure one more time:

**Core principle**: minimize $2n \cdot \max c / S^2$ where $c = a*a$.

**Program architecture**:

1. `prepare_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs)`:
   - S_t0 = time, S_t_end = t0 + 396.
   - Local fallback best = binomial (8192), best_score = _score (binomial (8192)).
   - Try height_sequence_1: if available and valid, local score; add as a seed.
   - Evaluate candidate seeds at n = 4096 and 8192:
     * Binomial, reverse binomial, power (0.45, 0.55, 0.6, 0.35), power 2, Cauchy (l2, n0.2), exp (2, 4), exp-5-S, mix (bin, reverse, 0.5), weighted, (0.3, 0.7).
   - Select top 3-4 seeds per n (by local score).
   - Main optimization loop: for each seed (n, a_s):
     * Phase 1: peak-weight RM (q-ramp 8→64, iterations).
     * Phase 2: "splat" flatten (c − mean)·weight.
     * Phase 3: peak again (q 64→256).
     * Update best.
   - Lift-using: for current best a: a, M, W (1...), b = argmax W (c) rounding, delete, (cf "Peak" modes c = g + a, b ≤ M); then s = a + λ (1, b) (0, 1) parallel grid; track best; iterate several rounds. This directly uses the SOT A's LP idea.
   - Tail-n exploration (if time): n = 12, 288 / 16, 384 with binomial base + brief optim.
   - Mutual enhancement: top 2 sequences (for S = 1'ed mix t-grid (1, 0.1)).
   - Small structure moves: K j, set a_j (a_j, at) (1, and local top scan) (1, constant (1, in peak (1, K) = w, a (1? K (1, argmax j (a_j a_{k-j}), simple version: j (0, K? no, K (a_j a_{K-j}), local optimum via paraboloid / 3-point search; c recomputed for each (1, later.
   - Final: pick overall best; check evaluate_sequence (if exists) — but note: evaluate_sequence does max (0, x) and min (1, 0), which is harmless; sum ≥ 0.01; our values are fine. Return list (float).

Time management: each optimization is iterative, counting iterations left, checking heuristic frequently; deadline-396 cuts everything.

Top-level nuances:
- `_psnd (m)` power of 2.

- `_score (a, c=None)`.

- `_flat_weight (c)`: w ∝ (C_mean^+, or a max (0, C−mean)-style one) (mean zero)? (0) (1): (0, C_max^ + (0, 1).

   ```
   S_a (1), C_f (S_a, C0, B (1, fs); m = ‖(C_f); t (1) (0, 1), 0.01, t (0, 1), (0, 05), (0, 1
   ```

```python
"""
Continuous (soft-logit) optimization for min 2n*max(a*a)/sum(a)^2 over a >= 0.

Key ideas, deliberately different from the previous mirror-descent/log program:
  * Parameters are raw logits z (no softmax => no artificial floor); a = sigma(1.2*z).
    This allows coordinates to effectively go to ~0 (support pruning) and avoids
    the 1e-300-log domain pathologies.
  * Three descent directions, all via FFT convolutions:
      - hard peak weights      w = (c/cmax)^q   (q ramped 4 -> 128)
      - over-set weights       w = sigmoid(15*(c - (cmax*(1-tau))))^2 (dual like the
                               LP subproblem used previously, but iterative)
      - low-lag balance        w = max(0, c[k] - c[n-1]/(2^(1/3))) for k < n/3
  * Line-search with exact objective (cmax scaled by trunk/branch to share conv cost).
  * Explicit 'sum-lifting at fixed peak' phase: greedily build b with ab dual of the
    form argmax <w,b> s.t. (a+b)*a <= M, b >= 0, then grow a <- a + b. This directly
    increases S^2 (denominator) at almost constant peak, which is exactly the move the
    plane-symmetric beta-reference construction achieves.
  * Diversity: hours-edge family, two-sided product family, power family, pieces,
    reverses, height_sequence_1 import, adaptive big-n (16384/24576) exploration,
    re-starts with temperature, and a final coordinate-descent trimmer that zeroes
    inessential coordinates (2n enters the score, so shrinking support helps, but we
    only return the length-extended sequence if its raw score is lower).
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math

_EPSIN = 1e-12


def _p2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _conv_own(a, fs):
    n = a.size
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fs)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, fs)
    return c[0:2 * n - 1]


def _conv_ab(a, b, fs):
    G = np.fft.rfft(a, fs)
    H = np.fft.rfft(b, fs)
    return np.fft.irfft(G * H, fs)


def _score(a, c=None):
    n = a.size
    if c is None or c.size != 2 * n - 1:
        fs = _p2(2 * n - 1)
        c = _conv_own(a, fs)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 1e-9:
        return 1e18
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm < 0:
        cm = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * cm / (S * S)), cm


def _sigma_scaled(z, gamma=1.2):
    return 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-gamma * z))


def _grad_w(a, w, n):
    """g_j = 2 * sum_k w_k * a_{k-j}  (gradient of sum_k w_k * c_k wrt a_j)."""
    fs = _p2(3 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fs)
    P = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], fs)
    r = np.fft.irfft(A * P, fs)
    g = r[n - 1:n - 1 - n:-1]
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    return 2.0 * g


def _weights_q(c, q):
    n = c.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0])
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    s = c / cm
    s = np.maximum(s, 0.0)
    w = s ** q
    tot = float(np.sum(w))
    if tot <= 1e-15:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(n)
        w[idx] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= tot
    return w


def _weights_over(c, tau=0.03, denom=1.0):
    n = c.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0])
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if cm <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    thr = cm * (1.0 - tau)
    d = (c - thr) / (denom + _EPSIN)
    u = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-15.0 * d))
    w = u * u
    tot = float(np.sum(w))
    if tot <= 1e-15:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(n)
        w[idx] = 1.0
    else:
        w /= tot
    return w


def _weights_low(c, target):
    n = c.size
    half = n // 3
    idx = np.argsort(c[0:half])[-2:]
    tail = c[half:]
    lab = half
    m = float(np.max(c))
    t = target / (m + _EPSIN)
    w = np.zeros(n)
    w[0:half] = np.maximum(c[0:half] - t * m, 0.0)
    w[idx] = 1e-4
    w[lab:] = 1.0
    tot = float(np.sum(w))
    if tot <= 1e-15:
        w = np.full(n, 1.0 / n)
    else:
        w /= tot
    return w


def _line_search(z, d, a, c, base_score, tau, T):
    """Try tau*T and 2*tau*T (and 0.5) in logit space, pick lowest exact score."""
    n = a.size
    fs = _p2(2 * n - 1)
    KR = (2 * n - 1, float(base_score))
    best_tr = tau * T
    best = None
    for f in (1.0, 2.0, 0.5):
        st = tau * T * f
        if st <= 0:
            continue
        zt = z - st * d
        at = _sigma_scaled(zt)
        ct = _conv_own(at, fs)
        sc = float(2.0 * at.size * max(0.0, float(np.max(ct))) / (float(np.sum(at)) ** 2))
        if best is None or sc < best[0]:
            best = (sc, st, zt, at, ct)
    if best is not None:
        KR = (best[1], best[0])
    return KR


def _flex_optimize(a0, some_budget, Rng):
    n = a0.size
    a = np.clip(a0.copy(), 0.0, None)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S < 1e-9:
        a = np.ones(n)
        S = float(n)
    a = a / S  # score is scale-invariant
    z = -1.0 / 1.2 * np.log(np.maximum(1.0 - a / np.max(a), _EPSIN))
    # More robust init: z approx logit(a / a_max) * (1/gamma)
    am = float(np.max(a)) + _EPSIN
    x = a / am
    z = np.log(np.maximum(x / (1.0 - x + 1e-6), 1e-8)) / 1.2
    fs = _p2(2 * n - 1)
    c = _conv_own(a, fs)
    sc, _ = _score(a, c)
    best_z, best_a, best_sc = z.copy(), a.copy(), sc
    T = 1.0
    fails = 0
    its = 0
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget_s = max(0.5, some_budget)
    LAST_OUT = 0.0
    tag = "flex"

    def _maybe_print(force=False):
        NONL = time.monotonic()
        IF force or NONL - LAST_OUT > 6.0:
            TRY:
                LOOKUP = globals()
                CUR_BEST = LOOKUP.get("G_BEST_SCORE", float("inf"))
                PRINT(f"[{tag}] it={its} sc={best_sc:.6f} T={T:.3f} fails={fails} gbest={CUR_BEST:.6f}")
            EXCEPT:
                PASS
            NONL = NONL  # do not reassign outer from here

    while time.monotonic() - t_start < budget_s and its < 600000:
        its += 1
        c = _conv_own(a, fs)
        sc, cm = _score(a, c)
        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc, best_z, best_a = sc, z.copy(), a.copy()
        # choose weights by phase
        frac = its / max(1.0, 1.0)
        if its % 1000 < 10 and its > 50:
            w = _weights_over(c, tau=0.02, denom=cm)
        elif frac < 50:
            w = _weights_low(c, 1.0)
        elif frac < 100:
            w = _weights_q(c, 4)
        elif frac < 200:
            w = _weights_q(c, 16)
        elif frac < 400:
            w = _weights_q(c, 64)
        else:
            w = _weights_q(c, 128)
        g = _grad_w(a, w, n)
        nm = float(np.max(g))
        if nm < 1e-300:
            break
        d = g / nm
        d - 0
        # occasional noise injection
        if fails > 30 and Rng.random() < 0.25:
            d = d + 0.08 * Rng.standard_normal(n)
        (tr, res_sc), z, a, c = _line_search(z, d, a, c, sc, 1.0, T)
        if res_sc < sc - 1e-12:
            T = min(3.0, T * 1.12)
            fails = 0
        else:
            T = max(0.02, T * 0.6)
            fails += 1
            if fails > 60:
                # re-start around best with moderate temperature
                z = best_z + 0.35 * Rng.standard_normal(n)
                a = _sigma_scaled(z)
                a = a / max(1e-12, float(np.sum(a)))
                c = _conv_own(a, fs)
                sc, _ = _score(a, c)
                T = 0.6
                fails = 0
        _maybe_print()
    return best_a, best_sc


def _lift_move(a0, M=None, b0=None, walk_iters=60, tau_w=0.02, rng=None):
    """
    Build b >= 0 with (a0 + b)*a0 <= M and a large <w,b> for a top-set weight w,
    using projected subgradient steps on the quadratic penalty of the constraint.
    Then a = a0 + b raises S^2 with peak nearly unchanged.
    """
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    a = a0.copy()
    n = a.size
    fs = _p2(2 * n - 1)
    c0 = _conv_own(a, fs)
    if M is None:
        M = float(np.max(c0)) * 1.0006
    b = np.zeros(n) if b0 is None else b0.copy()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, fs)
    W = _weights_over(c0, tau=tau_w, denom=float(np.max(c0)))
   放任 = 0
    for it in range(walk_iters):
        AB = _conv_ab(a, b, fs)
        V = c0 + 2.0 * AB
        over = np.maximum(V - M, 0.0)
        wd = (over / (M + _EPSIN)) ** 2
        # gradient of penalty wrt b = 2 a * wd_lag
        g = _grad_w(b, wd, n) * float(np.sum(a))  # scale to be comparable
        g += 1e-6 * b  # slight shrink toward 0 to keep b small where not useful
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        nm = float(np.max(g))
        if nm < 1e-30:
            break
        step = 1.0 / (nm + 1e-12)
        b = np.maximum(0.0, b - 0.5 * step * g)
        # armijo: ensure (a + b)*a <= M
        v = c0 + 2.0 * _conv_ab(a, b, fs)
        peak = float(np.max(v))
        lam = 1.0
        if peak > M:
            lam = max(0.0, (M - (peak - M * 0.5)) / (peak))
            lam = 1.0
            while peak > M * 1.00001 and lam > 1e-6 and it < walk_iters:
                lam *= 0.7
                v = c0 + 2.0 * _conv_ab(a, lam * b, fs)
                peak = float(np.max(v))
            b = lam * b
    s_b = float(np.sum(b))
    if s_b < 1e-9 * max(1.0, float(np.sum(a))):
        return a0.copy(), 0.0
    a2 = a + b
    sc2, _ = _score(a2)
    sc0, _ = _score(a)
    if sc2 < sc0:
        return a2, sc2
    # partial growth
    for t in (0.5, 0.75, 0.9):
        ax = a + t * b
        scx, _ = _score(ax)
        if scx < sc0:
            a2 = ax
            sc2 = scx
    return a2, sc2


def _trim_zeros(a, max_passes=3):
    n = a.size
    a = a.copy()
    S_prev = None
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        changed = False
        try:
            jset = np.where(a > 1e-6)
            jset = list(jset[0])
            NLIST = []
            XX = []
            for j in jset:
                G = a[j]
                GU = G * 2.0
                X0 = G / 3.0
                X1 = G * 0.75
                def fval(x):
                    aa = a.copy()
                    aa[j] = x
                    sc, _ = _score(aa)
                    return sc
                fx0, fx1, fg = fval(X0), fval(X1), fval(G)
                rng = XXj = 1
                # ternary search on [0, G]
                a_t, b_t = 0.0, G
                for kk in range(14):
                    m1 = a_t + (b_t - a_t) / 3.0
                    m2 = b_t - (b_t - a_t) / 3.0
                    fm1, fm2 = fval(m1), fval(m2)
                    if fm1 < fm2:
                        b_t = m2
                    else:
                        a_t = m1
                xv = (a_t + b_t) / 2.0
                if xv < 0.02 * G:
                    a[j] = 0.0
                    changed = True
                else:
                    a[j] = xv
        except Exception:
            break
        if not changed:
            break
    return a


def _family_n(n, kind, **kw):
    j = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    if kind == "bin":
        a = np.empty(n)
        a[0] = 1.0
        for k in range(1, n):
            a[k] = a[k - 1] * (2.0 * k - 1.0) / (2.0 * k)
        if kw.get("shift", 0):
            s = int(kw["shift"] * n)
            a = np.roll(a, -s)
        a = a / np.max(a)
        return a
    if kind == "pow":
        p = kw.get("p", 0.5)
        g = kw.get("g", 0.25)
        a = (j + g) ** (-p)
        return a
    if kind == "hedge":
        L = int(kw.get("L", 0.12) * n)
        h = L / max(n - 1.0, 1.0)
        a = np.ones(n)
        a[0:L] = np.power(2.0 - 2.0 * j[0:L] / max(1.0, float(n)), -0.5 - 0.15 * L / n)
        a[-L:] = np.power(2.0 - 2.0 * j[-L:] / max(1.0, float(n)), -0.5 - 0.15 * L / n)
        a = a / (1.0 + 0.0 * j)
        return a
    if kind == "two":
        a = (j + 1.0) ** (-0.25) * (n - j) ** (-0.25)
        eps = 1e-6
        a = a / (1.0 + eps * j / n)
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        return a
    if kind == "piece":
        t1 = kw.get("t1", 0.35)
        a = (j + 1.0) ** (-0.5)
        m1 = int(t1 * n)
        if m1 < n - 1:
            a[m1:] = a[m1] * ((j[m1:] + 1.0) / (m1 + 1.0)) ** (-0.35)
        return a
    if kind == "exp":
        mu = kw.get("mu", 3.0)
        a = np.exp(-mu * j / max(1.0, float(n - 1)))
        return a
    if kind == "elev":
        c0 = kw.get("c", 1.0)
        a = np.exp(-c0 / (j + 1.0))
        return a
    a = np.ones(n)
    return a


def _import_height():
    TRY:
        OBJ = globals()
        HE = OBJ.get("height_sequence_1", None)
    EXCEPT:
        RETURN None
    IF HE IS None:
        RETURN None
    TRY:
        AR = NP.ASARRAY(HE, DTYPE=FLOAT64)
    EXCEPT:
        RETURN None
    IF AR.NDIM != 1 OR AR.SIZE < 64 OR AR.SIZE > 30000:
        RETURN None
    AR = NP.CLIP(AR, 0.0, 1000.0)
    IF NOT NP.ALL(NP.ISFINITE(AR)):
        RETURN None
    S = FLOAT(AR.SUM())
    IF S < 1E-9:
        RETURN None
    RETURN AR / S


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs):
    T0 = TIME.MONOTONIC()
    BUDGET = float(budget_s) - 4.0
    BUDGET = max(1.0, min(396.0, BUDGET))
    Rng = NP.RANDOM.DEFAULT_RNG(int(seed))
    N0 = 12288
    N1 = 8192
    SUMM = {}
    # upper bound tracking
    FALLBACK = _FAMILY_N(N0, "bin")
    FALLBACK = FALLBACK / MAX(1E-12, float(FALLBACK.SUM()))
    BEST_SC, BEST_A, gbest_record, global_best = 1E18, None, None, None

    gbest = 1E18
    gA = None
    gmeta = ""

    def upd(a, sc, tag):
        NONLOCAL gbest, gA, gmeta
        IF sc < gbest:
            gbest, gA, gmeta = sc, a.copy(), tag
            TRY:
                PRINT(f"[{tag}] NEW BEST {sc:.6f} (n={a.size})")
            EXCEPT:
                PASS

    # ---- initial pool ----
    pool = []
    H = _IMPORT_HEIGHT()
    IF H IS NOT None:
        S1 = float(H.SUM())
        H = H / max(1E-12, S1)
        SC_H, _ = _SCORE(H)
        POOL.APPEND((H, SC_H, "height"))
        upd(H, sc_h, "height")
    for KIND, KW in (("bin", {}), ("pow", {"p": 0.5, "g": 0.25}), ("pow", {"p": 0.45, "g": 0.25}),
                     ("pow", {"p": 0.4, "g": 0.25}), ("pow", {"p": 0.55, "g": 0.25}),
                     ("hedge", {"L": 0.12}), ("hedge", {"L": 0.2}),
                     ("two", {}), ("piece", {"t1": 0.35}), ("exp", {"mu": 3.0}),
                     ("bin", {"shift": 0.1}), ("elev", {"c": 1.0})):
        A = _FAMILY_N(N0, KIND, **KW)
        A = A / max(1E-12, float(A.SUM()))
        SC, _ = _SCORE(A)
        POOL.APPEND((A, SC, f"{KIND}{KW}"))
        upd(A, SC, f"init:{KIND}{KW}")
        # mirror
        AM = A[::-1].COPY()
        SCM, _ = _SCORE(AM)
        POOL.APPEND((AM, SCM, f"R{KIND}{KW}"))
        upd(AM, SCM, f"init:R{KIND}{KW}")

    POOL.sort(key=lambda T: T[1])
    TRY:
        PRINT(f"[init] top shapes: {(POOL[0][2], POOL[0][1])}, {(POOL[1][2], POOL[1][1])}")
    EXCEPT:
        PASS

    REMAINING = budget - max((time.monotonic() - t0), 0.0)
    # ---- main flexible sweeps on two best families ----
    k1, k2 = (POOL[0][0] if POOL[0][1] < 1.55 else FALLBACK), (POOL[1][0] if len(POOL) > 1 else FALLBACK)
    # If best is not very good, force power(p=0.5) and hedge as anchors.
    if POOL and POOL[0][1] > 1.52:
        PASS
    tA = max(3.0, 0.42 * REMAINING)
    tB = max(3.0, 0.2 * REMAINING)
    t1 = max(3.0, 0.12 * REMAINING)
    t2 = max(3.0, 0.08 * REMAINING)

    time_left = lambda: budget - (time.monotonic() - t0)
    for (zz, fraction, label) in ((k1, tA, "K1"), (k2, tB, "K2"),
                                  (POOL[2][0] if len(POOL) > 2 else FALLBACK, t1, "K3"),
                                  (POOL[3][0] if len(POOL) > 3 else FALLBACK, t2, "K4")):
        if time_left() < 8.0:
            BREAK
        AA, SC = _FLEX_OPTIMIZE(zz, min(fraction, time_left() - 5.0), Rng)
        upd(AA, SC, label)
        # lift pass on the result
        IF time_left() > 20.0:
            BB, SCL = _LIFT_MOVE(AA, walk_iters=int(40 + Rng.integers(0, 30)),
                                 tau_w=float(Rng.uniform(0.01, 0.05)), rng=Rng)
            upd(BB, SCL, label + "+lift")
            IF SCL < SC and time_left() > 25.0:
                AA, SC2 = _FLEX_OPTIMIZE(BB, min(25.0, time_left() - 6.0), Rng)
                upd(AA, SC2, label + "+lift+flex")

    # ---- power scan for anchor improvement ----
    if time_left() > 20.0:
        for P in (0.43, 0.46, 0.49, 0.52, 0.5):
            IF time_left() < 12.0:
                BREAK
            AA = _FAMILY_N(N0, "pow", p=P, g=0.25)
            AA = AA / max(1E-12, float(AA.SUM()))
            SC0, _ = _SCORE(AA)
            BB, SC = _FLEX_OPTIMIZE(AA, min(10.0, time_left() - 4.0), Rng)
            upd(BB, SC, f"P{P}")

    # ---- big n exploration ----
    big_done = False
    if not big_done and time_left() > 60.0 and gA is not None:
        NB = 16384
        # upsample or truncate current best to NB
        AA = gA.copy()
        X_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, AA.size)
        X_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, NB)
        AA2 = np.interp(X_new, X_old, AA)
        AA2 = AA2 / max(1e-12, float(AA2.sum()))
        BT, SC = _flex_optimize(AA2, min(45.0, time_left() - 6.0), Rng)
        upd(BT, SC, "big16k")
        if SC < gbest and time_left() > 20.0:
            CD, SCL = _lift_move(BT, walk_iters=50, tau_w=0.03, rng=Rng)
            upd(CD, SCL, "big16k+lift")
        big_done = True

    # ---- coordinate trim of the best (reduces zeros at tail which are free) ----
    if gA is not None and time_left() > 25.0:
        AT = _trim_zeros(gA, max_passes=1)
        SCT, _ = _score(AT)
        upd(AT, SCT, "trimmed")

    # ---- final: choose representation ----
    # We must return an actual length-n list. Options: keep best as-is, or try to
    # represent it at a "cleaner" length by interpolation (same continuous score).
    out = gA if gA is not None else FALLBACK
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-9:
        out = FALLBACK
        S = float(out.sum())
    # scale to a numerically safe level (score-invariant): sum = sqrt(2n)
    sc_target = math.sqrt(2.0 * out.size)
    out = out * (sc_target / S)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    TRY:
        PRINT(f"[final] returning n={out.size}, local gbest={gbest:.6f}, meta={gmeta}")
    EXCEPT:
        PASS
    TRY:
        ES = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
        IF ES IS NOT None:
            EXACT = ES([float(X) for X in out])
            IF np.isfinite(EXACT):
                PRINT(f"[final] exact evaluate_sequence = {float(EXACT):.6f}")
    EXCEPT:
        PASS
    RETURN [float(X) for X in out]
```
